matching the DynamoDB `STATE` projection (so `GET /v1/settlements/{id}` immediately after `deploy.sh` hits cache and returns `X-ClearLedger-Source: cache` with the exact same payload as a DynamoDB projection read).\n\n=== s3\n# S3 Audit Archive (`services/s3.md`)\n\n- Provision a dedicated S3 bucket (`aws_s3_bucket`) for NDJSON audit batches (`force_destroy = true` recommended so `destroy.sh` can remove the bucket after versioned audit objects and delete markers are written).\n- Enable bucket versioning (`aws_s3_bucket_versioning` with `status = \"Enabled\"`).\n- Enable default server-side encryption (`aws_s3_bucket_server_side_encryption_configuration`) using `sse_algorithm = \"aws:kms\"` and `kms_master_key_id = kms.audit_arn`.\n- Block all public access (`aws_s3_bucket_public_access_block`) with all four flags (`block_public_acls`, `block_public_policy`, `ignore_public_acls`, `restrict_public_buckets`) set to `true`.\n- Attach a bucket resource policy (`aws_s3_bucket_policy`) to the audit bucket that:\n  - Explicitly allows (`Effect = \"Allow\"`) `iam.archiver_role_arn` to perform `s3:PutObject`, `s3:GetObject`, and `s3:AbortMultipartUpload` on `<bucket_arn>/ledger-audit/*` and `s3:ListBucket`, `s3:GetBucketLocation` on `<bucket_arn>`.\n  - Explicitly denies (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `s3:DeleteObject` and `s3:DeleteObjectVersion` on `<bucket_arn>` and `<bucket_arn>/*` for all six ClearLedger workload IAM role ARNs (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`), and explicitly denies (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `s3:PutObject` on `<bucket_arn>` and `<bucket_arn>/*` for the five non-archiver ClearLedger workload IAM role ARNs (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `scheduler`).\n- Configure the Audit Archiver Lambda with `AUDIT_BUCKET = <bucket_name>` and `AUDIT_PREFIX = \"ledger-audit/\"` (`manifest.audit.prefix = \"ledger-audit/\"`).\n- `clearledger-audit-archiver` writes NDJSON batches to `s3://<bucket_name>/ledger-audit/batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`, where `<first_seq:08d>` and `<last_seq:08d>` are the 8-digit zero-padded first and last `clearledger.outbox.seq` in the batch, each batch contains a contiguous, gap-free slice of all committed `clearledger.outbox` rows whose `seq` lies in `[first_seq, last_seq]` ordered by strictly ascending `clearledger.outbox.seq`, batch `[first_seq, last_seq]` intervals are strictly disjoint and non-overlapping across objects, each line is serialized in canonical `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` field order (`schemaVersion` first, `data.kind` first), and `<16_char_sha256_hex>` is the first 16 lowercase hexadecimal characters of the SHA-256 digest of the object's raw bytes.\n- During `deploy.sh` data-plane convergence, reconcile the versioned S3 audit bucket against `clearledger.outbox` so that all objects reside under `ledger-audit/`, every batch object has a valid canonical key (`batch-<first_seq:08d>-<last_seq:08d>-<16_char_sha256_hex>.ndjson`), valid SHA-256 digest, contiguous gap-free `clearledger.outbox.seq` coverage across `[first_seq, last_seq]`, and non-overlapping `[first_seq, last_seq]` bounds across batches, every committed event in `clearledger.outbox` appears **exactly once** across the S3 audit batches with its canonical `ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope` serialization and `archived_at IS NOT NULL` in PostgreSQL, and all noncurrent object versions (`IsLatest = false`) and delete markers are permanently purged from the versioned bucket so only the single current version (`IsLatest = true`) of each valid canonical batch remains.\n=== cognito\n# Cognito User Pool and OAuth2 Scopes (`services/cognito.md`)\n\n- Provision a Cognito User Pool (`aws_cognito_user_pool`) (an `aws_cognito_user_pool_domain` is optional).\n- Provision a Cognito Resource Server (`aws_cognito_resource_server`) with:\n  - `identifier = \"clearledger\"` (`manifest.auth.resource_server_identifier = \"clearledger\"`)\n  - Three scopes: `read`, `write`, and `admin` (yielding full scope strings `clearledger/read`, `clearledger/write`, and `clearledger/admin`).\n- Provision three separate Cognito User Pool Clients (`aws_cognito_user_pool_client`), each configured with:\n  - `generate_secret = true`\n  - `allowed_oauth_flows_user_pool_client = true`\n  - `allowed_oauth_flows = [\"client_credentials\"]`\n  - Exact single scope per client:\n    - `clients.read`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/read\"]`\n    - `clients.write`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/write\"]`\n    - `clients.admin`: `allowed_oauth_scopes = [\"clearledger/admin\"]`\n- Export `issuer_url` (`http://aws:4566/<user_pool_id>`) and `token_endpoint` (`http://aws:4566/cognito-idp/oauth2/token` or `http://aws:4566/<domain>.auth.<region>.amazoncognito.com/oauth2/token`) in `manifest.json`.\n=== iam\n# IAM Roles and Least-Privilege Policies (`services/iam.md`)\n\nProvision six distinct IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with role-specific inline or attached policies (`aws_iam_role_policy` or `aws_iam_policy` + `aws_iam_role_policy_attachment`).\n\n## Global Least-Privilege Rules\n\n- Tag each of the six IAM roles (`aws_iam_role`) with `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` and `ClearLedgerRole = <ecs_execution|ecs_task|projector|relay|archiver|scheduler>`.\n- Never use wildcard actions (`Action = \"*\"` or `service:*`) or wildcard resources (`Resource = \"*\"`) in any `Allow` statement across any role policy.\n- **Per-workload CloudWatch Log Group isolation**: Each logging workload (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`) may only grant CloudWatch Logs write permissions (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) on its **own** dedicated CloudWatch Log Group ARN (`arn:aws:logs:<region>:<account>:log-group:/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/<workload>*`). Do not use a shared `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/*` or `/clearledger/*` log group wildcard that permits one workload to write into another workload's log group, and do not grant any CloudWatch Logs actions to `scheduler_role_arn`.\n- **Strict KMS key isolation across all six roles**: Each workload may only be granted `kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, and `kms:DescribeKey` on the specific customer-managed KMS keys required for the data stores it accesses at runtime (`relay` and `archiver` on `database`; `ecs_task`, `projector`, and `relay` on `messaging`; `ecs_task` and `projector` on `projection`; `archiver` on `audit`). No role may grant `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on any of the other KMS keys, `ecs_execution_role_arn` and `scheduler_role_arn` must not grant access to any of the four KMS keys, and every role must explicitly deny (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four customer-managed KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`).\n- **Defense-in-depth explicit `Deny` guardrail statements**: Do not rely solely on implicit omission for forbidden permissions. Every role's policy document must include explicit `Effect = \"Deny\"` statement(s) covering `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` on all four KMS keys as well as all forbidden actions and resources listed under **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)** for that role below.\n- **Out-of-band IAM policy reconciliation in `deploy.sh`**: During `deploy.sh`, any out-of-band inline policies or attached managed policies added to any of the six ClearLedger IAM roles outside Terraform/OpenTofu must be removed (and unattached `<resource_prefix>`-scoped local customer-managed IAM policies deleted) so that each role retains only its canonical least-privilege policy.\n\n## Role Responsibilities and Access Boundaries\n\n1. **`ecs_execution_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com` (and no other service or wildcard principal).\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**: CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing (`logs:CreateLogStream`, `logs:PutLogEvents`, `logs:DescribeLogStreams`) scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on the audit bucket (`<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`), `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on the three non-API CloudWatch Log Groups (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n2. **`ecs_task_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `ecs-tasks.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Read-only access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:Query`, `dynamodb:DescribeTable`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.api_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, consuming or deleting SQS messages (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; mutating DynamoDB (`dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-API log group (`projector`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n3. **`projector_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Consume-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:ChangeMessageVisibility`) and dead-letter forwarding (`sqs:SendMessage`) on the DLQ ARN.\n     - Append/monotonic-upsert access on the projection DynamoDB table ARN and its specific `AccountIndex` GSI ARN `<table_arn>/index/AccountIndex` (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:Query`; do not use `<table_arn>/index/*` or `<table_arn>/*` wildcards).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.projector_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:SendMessage` on the main queue or `sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ; deleting DynamoDB items (`dynamodb:DeleteItem`) or tables (`dynamodb:DeleteTable`) on the projection table; any S3 access (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`; `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `database` and `audit` KMS keys; and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-projector log group (`api`, `relay`, `archiver`). Furthermore, must not grant `dynamodb:Query` on `<table_arn>/index/*`.\n4. **`relay_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Publish-only access on the main SQS queue ARN (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:GetQueueAttributes`, `sqs:GetQueueUrl`).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `messaging` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.relay_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage` or `sqs:DeleteMessage` on the main queue, `sqs:SendMessage`/`sqs:ReceiveMessage`/`sqs:DeleteMessage` on the DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `projection` and `audit` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-relay log group (`api`, `projector`, `archiver`).\n5. **`archiver_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `lambda.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - Append-only object access (`s3:PutObject`, `s3:GetObject`, `s3:AbortMultipartUpload`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>/ledger-audit/*`, and bucket-level metadata (`s3:ListBucket`, `s3:GetBucketLocation`) scoped strictly to `<audit_bucket_arn>` (keep object-level and bucket-level statements separated by resource type).\n     - Cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) on the `audit` and `database` KMS key ARNs only.\n     - CloudWatch Logs stream creation/writing scoped strictly to `logs.archiver_log_group`.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not grant `s3:PutObject` on the bucket ARN `<audit_bucket_arn>` or unscoped `<audit_bucket_arn>/*` root wildcard, and must not grant `s3:ListBucket` on object ARNs. Must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"` `s3:DeleteObject` and `s3:DeleteObjectVersion` on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on the `messaging` and `projection` KMS keys, and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on any non-archiver log group (`api`, `projector`, `relay`).\n6. **`scheduler_role_arn`**:\n   - **Trust principal**: `scheduler.amazonaws.com`.\n   - **Allowed access (`Effect = \"Allow\"`)**:\n     - `lambda:InvokeFunction` scoped strictly to the Outbox Relay (`workers.outbox_relay.function_arn`) and Audit Archiver (`workers.audit_archiver.function_arn`) Lambda function ARNs.\n   - **Forbidden access (`Effect = \"Deny\"` required)**: Must not allow, and must explicitly deny via `Effect = \"Deny\"`, `lambda:InvokeFunction` on the Projector Lambda (`workers.projector.function_arn`), any SQS actions (`sqs:SendMessage`, `sqs:ReceiveMessage`, `sqs:DeleteMessage`) on the main queue or DLQ, any DynamoDB actions (`dynamodb:GetItem`, `dynamodb:PutItem`, `dynamodb:UpdateItem`, `dynamodb:DeleteItem`, `dynamodb:Query`) on the projection table or `AccountIndex`, any S3 actions (`s3:GetObject`, `s3:PutObject`, `s3:DeleteObject`, `s3:ListBucket`) on `<audit_bucket_arn>` and `<audit_bucket_arn>/*`, `kms:Decrypt` or `kms:GenerateDataKey` on all four KMS keys (`database`, `messaging`, `projection`, `audit`), and `logs:CreateLogStream` or `logs:PutLogEvents` on all four CloudWatch Log Groups (`api`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`).\n=== kms\n# Customer-Managed KMS Keys (`services/kms.md`)\n\nProvision four separate customer-managed KMS keys (`aws_kms_key`) and four KMS aliases (`aws_kms_alias`):\n\n- `kms.database_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-database`: encrypts the RDS PostgreSQL instance\n- `kms.messaging_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-messaging`: encrypts the main SQS queue and DLQ\n- `kms.projection_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-projection`: encrypts the DynamoDB projection table\n- `kms.audit_arn` with alias `alias/<resource_prefix>-audit`: encrypts the S3 audit archive bucket\n\nEach of the four KMS keys must be configured with:\n- `enable_key_rotation = true`\n- `deletion_window_in_days = 10` (must be `10..30` days)\n- Tags `ClearLedgerDeployment = <resource_prefix>` and `ClearLedgerKeyUsage = <database|messaging|projection|audit>` (matching its respective role: `database`, `messaging`, `projection`, or `audit`)\n- **Resource-based KMS key policy (`aws_kms_key.policy` or `aws_kms_key_policy`)**:\n  - Grants key administration to the AWS account root principal (`arn:aws:iam::000000000000:root`).\n  - Explicitly allows (`Effect = \"Allow\"`) cryptographic usage (`kms:Decrypt`, `kms:GenerateDataKey`, `kms:DescribeKey`) to the exact ClearLedger workload IAM role ARNs authorized for that key:\n    - `database`: `iam.relay_role_arn` and `iam.archiver_role_arn`\n    - `messaging`: `iam.ecs_task_role_arn`, `iam.projector_role_arn`, and `iam.relay_role_arn`\n    - `projection`: `iam.ecs_task_role_arn` and `iam.projector_role_arn`\n    - `audit`: `iam.archiver_role_arn`\n  - Explicitly denies (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:DisableKey` and `kms:ScheduleKeyDeletion` when the principal is any of the six ClearLedger workload IAM role ARNs (`ecs_execution`, `ecs_task`, `projector`, `relay`, `archiver`, `scheduler`), and explicitly denies (`Effect = \"Deny\"`) `kms:Decrypt` and `kms:GenerateDataKey` when the principal is any of the ClearLedger workload IAM role ARNs not authorized for that key.\n- Re-running `deploy.sh` must ensure all four KMS keys remain enabled (`KeyState = \"Enabled\"`), have `enable_key_rotation = true`, retain their canonical `ClearLedgerDeployment` and `ClearLedgerKeyUsage` tags, and enforce their canonical key policies.\n=== cloudwatch-logs\n# CloudWatch Log Groups (`services/cloudwatch-logs.md`)\n\nProvision four dedicated CloudWatch Log Groups (`aws_cloudwatch_log_group`), each with `retention_in_days = 14` (at least `14` days):\n\n- `logs.api_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/api`\n- `logs.projector_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/projector`\n- `logs.relay_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/outbox-relay`\n- `logs.archiver_log_group`: `/clearledger/<resource_prefix>/audit-archiver`\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"cd /workspace/contracts/services && for f in dynamodb elasticache-valkey s3 cognito iam kms cloudwatch-logs; do echo \"=== $f\"; cat $f.md; done","time":{"start":1791658824741,"end":1791658824842}},"metadata":{"anthropic":{"caller":{"type":"direct"}}},"id":"prt_12730141a001fDMCa6QLegA2GH","sessionID":"ses_ed8d012a6ffe9CXzopqNwMtY2t","messageID":"msg_1273008d3001ewVqqFSr6FfVfQ"}}

false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_text(p->'aggregateId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'aggregateVersion') <> 'number'\n     OR (p->>'aggregateVersion') !~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF length(p->>'aggregateVersion') > 9 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer;\n  IF v < 1 THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(p->'occurredAt') <> 'string'\n     OR (p->>'occurredAt') !~ '^[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}T[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}(\\.[0-9]{1,9})?(Z|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})$'\n  THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  BEGIN\n    ts := (p->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz;\n  EXCEPTION WHEN others THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(p->'correlationId', 4, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(p->'idempotencyKey', 8, 128) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  d := p->'data';\n  IF d IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(d) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (ARRAY(SELECT jsonb_object_keys(d)) <@ data_keys) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (d ?& ARRAY['kind','accountId','reference','debitParty','creditParty','status','clearingStage']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'kind') <> 'string' OR d->>'kind' NOT IN ('settlementInitiated','ledgerEntryRecorded') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(d->'accountId', 3, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(d->'reference', 3, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(d->'debitParty', 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(d->'creditParty', 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d->>'debitParty' = d->>'creditParty' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'status') <> 'string'\n     OR d->>'status' NOT IN ('INITIATED','VALIDATED','RESERVED','CLEARED','SETTLED','RECONCILED','DISPUTED') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(d->'clearingStage') <> 'string' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'memo' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'memo') <> 'null'\n     AND NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(d->'memo', 1, 256) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF d ? 'entryId' AND jsonb_typeof(d->'entryId') <> 'null'\n     AND NOT clearledger.is_uuid_text(d->'entryId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n\n  -- version / kind / status / entryId coupling\n  IF v = 1 THEN\n    IF p->>'eventType' <> 'SettlementInitiated' OR d->>'kind' <> 'settlementInitiated' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF d->>'status' <> 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF d->>'clearingStage' IS DISTINCT FROM ('INITIATED@' || (d->>'debitParty')) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'memo') IS DISTINCT FROM 'Settlement initiated' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'entryId') IS NOT NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  ELSE\n    IF p->>'eventType' <> 'LedgerEntryRecorded' OR d->>'kind' <> 'ledgerEntryRecorded' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF d->>'status' = 'INITIATED' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.is_trimmed_text(d->'clearingStage', 2, 64) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n    IF (d->>'entryId') IS NULL THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN true;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Envelope validation plus column-to-envelope equality for clearledger.events\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_valid_event_row(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, c_event_type text,\n  c_correlation_id text, c_idempotency_key text, c_occurred_at timestamptz) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_valid_envelope(p) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'eventId')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM c_event_id THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'aggregateId')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM c_settlement_id THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer IS DISTINCT FROM c_version THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF p->>'eventType' IS DISTINCT FROM c_event_type THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF p->>'correlationId' IS DISTINCT FROM c_correlation_id THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF p->>'idempotencyKey' IS DISTINCT FROM c_idempotency_key THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'occurredAt')::timestamptz IS DISTINCT FROM c_occurred_at THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  RETURN true;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Envelope validation plus column-to-envelope equality for clearledger.outbox\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_valid_outbox_row(\n  p jsonb, c_event_id uuid, c_settlement_id uuid, c_version integer, c_correlation_id text) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_valid_envelope(p) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'eventId')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM c_event_id THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'aggregateId')::uuid IS DISTINCT FROM c_settlement_id THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF (p->>'aggregateVersion')::integer IS DISTINCT FROM c_version THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF p->>'correlationId' IS DISTINCT FROM c_correlation_id THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  RETURN true;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- Closed-schema WriteAcceptedResponse validation for clearledger.idempotency_keys\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.is_valid_idempotency_row(\n  c_scope text, c_status_code integer, r jsonb) RETURNS boolean\nLANGUAGE plpgsql IMMUTABLE AS $$\nDECLARE\n  v integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF c_scope IS NULL OR c_scope !~ '^(create|entry):[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' THEN\n    RETURN false;\n  END IF;\n  IF r IS NULL OR jsonb_typeof(r) <> 'object' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (ARRAY(SELECT jsonb_object_keys(r)) <@ ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT (r ?& ARRAY['settlementId','eventId','version','accepted','idempotentReplay']) THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_text(r->'settlementId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_uuid_text(r->'eventId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(r->'version') <> 'number' OR (r->>'version') !~ '^[0-9]{1,9}$' THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(r->'accepted') <> 'boolean' OR (r->'accepted') <> 'true'::jsonb THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF jsonb_typeof(r->'idempotentReplay') <> 'boolean' OR (r->'idempotentReplay') <> 'false'::jsonb THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  v := (r->>'version')::integer;\n  IF split_part(c_scope, ':', 2) <> (r->>'settlementId') THEN RETURN false; END IF;\n  IF split_part(c_scope, ':', 1) = 'create' THEN\n    RETURN c_status_code = 201 AND v = 1;\n  ELSE\n    RETURN c_status_code = 202 AND v >= 2;\n  END IF;\nEND\n$$;\n\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n-- Trigger functions\n-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.reject_mutation() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.%: % is not permitted (append-only ledger)', TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP\n    USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.settlements_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'INSERT' THEN\n    IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM 1 THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement must be initiated at version 1' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.last_memo IS NULL THEN\n      NEW.last_memo := 'Settlement initiated';\n    END IF;\n    RETURN NEW;\n  END IF;\n\n  -- UPDATE\n  IF OLD.current_status = 'RECONCILED' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % is RECONCILED (terminal)', OLD.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  NEW.last_memo := COALESCE(NEW.last_memo, OLD.last_memo);\n  IF NEW.settlement_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.settlement_id\n     OR NEW.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account_id\n     OR NEW.reference IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reference\n     OR NEW.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.debit_party\n     OR NEW.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.credit_party\n     OR NEW.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement header columns are immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.version IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.version + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement version must advance by exactly 1 (% -> %)', OLD.version, NEW.version USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.entry_count IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.entry_count + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement entry_count must advance by exactly 1' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.last_entry_id IS NULL OR NEW.last_entry_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_entry_id THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement update requires a new last_entry_id' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.updated_at IS NULL OR NOT (NEW.updated_at > OLD.updated_at) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement updated_at must strictly increase' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_valid_transition(OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid settlement status transition % -> %', OLD.current_status, NEW.current_status USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.events_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  s clearledger.settlements%ROWTYPE;\n  d jsonb;\n  prev_max integer;\n  prev_occurred timestamptz;\n  prev_status text;\n  expected_memo text;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP <> 'INSERT' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.events is append-only (% rejected)', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NOT clearledger.is_valid_event_row(NEW.payload, NEW.event_id, NEW.settlement_id, NEW.aggregate_version,\n        NEW.event_type, NEW.correlation_id, NEW.idempotency_key, NEW.occurred_at) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event payload does not conform to ClearLedgerDomainEventEnvelope' USING ERRCODE = '23514';\n  END IF;\n  d := NEW.payload->'data';\n\n  SELECT max(aggregate_version) INTO prev_max FROM clearledger.events WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version IS DISTINCT FROM COALESCE(prev_max, 0) + 1 THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event aggregate_version % is not contiguous (expected %)', NEW.aggregate_version, COALESCE(prev_max, 0) + 1\n      USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded' AND EXISTS (\n       SELECT 1 FROM clearledger.events e\n        WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n          AND e.event_type = 'LedgerEntryRecorded'\n          AND (e.payload->'data'->>'entryId') = (d->>'entryId')) THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'entryId % already recorded for settlement', d->>'entryId' USING ERRCODE = '23505';\n  END IF;\n\n  SELECT * INTO s FROM clearledger.settlements WHERE settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id;\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement % does not exist', NEW.settlement_id USING ERRCODE = '23503';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF s.account_id IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'accountId'\n     OR s.reference IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'reference'\n     OR s.debit_party IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'debitParty'\n     OR s.credit_party IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'creditParty'\n     OR s.current_status IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'status'\n     OR s.current_stage IS DISTINCT FROM d->>'clearingStage'\n     OR s.last_entry_id IS DISTINCT FROM (d->>'entryId')::uuid\n     OR s.version IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.aggregate_version\n     OR s.updated_at IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.occurred_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'event does not match settlement aggregate row' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF NEW.aggregate_version = 1 THEN\n    IF s.created_at IS DISTINCT FROM NEW.occurred_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'initiation event occurred_at must equal settlement created_at' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    SELECT e.occurred_at, e.payload->'data'->>'status' INTO prev_occurred, prev_status\n      FROM clearledger.events e\n     WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id AND e.aggregate_version = NEW.aggregate_version - 1;\n    IF NOT (NEW.occurred_at > prev_occurred) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'event occurred_at must be strictly after the preceding event' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NOT clearledger.is_valid_transition(prev_status, d->>'status') THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'invalid event status transition % -> %', prev_status, d->>'status' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n\n  IF (d->>'memo') IS NOT NULL THEN\n    expected_memo := d->>'memo';\n  ELSE\n    SELECT e.payload->'data'->>'memo' INTO expected_memo\n      FROM clearledger.events e\n     WHERE e.settlement_id = NEW.settlement_id\n       AND e.aggregate_version < NEW.aggregate_version\n       AND (e.payload->'data'->>'memo') IS NOT NULL\n     ORDER BY e.aggregate_version DESC\n     LIMIT 1;\n  END IF;\n  IF s.last_memo IS DISTINCT FROM expected_memo THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'settlement last_memo does not match event memo history' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.outbox_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  ev clearledger.events%ROWTYPE;\n  prev_max integer;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.outbox rows cannot be deleted' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF TG_OP = OLD.created_at THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox envelope columns are immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  IF NEW.attempts < OLD.attempts THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'outbox attempts cannot decrease' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n\n  IF OLD.published_at IS NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n    -- publishing an unpublished row\n    IF NOT (NEW.attempts > OLD.attempts) THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'publishing an outbox row must increment attempts' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'an outbox row OLD.published_at\n       OR NEW.attempts IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.attempts\n       OR NEW.last_error IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.last_error THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'published outbox delivery columns are immutable' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n    IF OLD.archived_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL\n       AND NEW.archived_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.archived_at THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'archived_at cannot be rewritten without first being reset' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSIF OLD.published_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.published_at IS NULL THEN\n    -- operational replay reset\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'replay reset requires archived_at = NULL' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  ELSE\n    -- still unpublished: failed delivery bookkeeping only\n    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL THEN\n      RAISE EXCEPTION 'unpublished outbox rows cannot be archived' USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n    END IF;\n  END IF;\n  RETURN NEW;\nEND\n$$;\n\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION clearledger.idempotency_guard() RETURNS trigger\nLANGUAGE plpgsql AS $$\nDECLARE\n  r jsonb;\n  ev_ok boolean;\n  ob_ok boolean;\nBEGIN\n  IF TG_OP <> 'INSERT' THEN\n    RAISE EXCEPTION 'clearledger.idempotency_keys is immutable (% rejected)', TG_OP USING ERRCODE = 'P0001';\n  END IF;\n  r := NEW.response_body;\n  IF NOT noqa: BLE001\n            log(f\"{fn.__name__}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n    wrapper.__name__ = fn.__name__\n    return wrapper\n\n\ndef attempt(desc, fn, *a, **kw):\n    try:\n        fn(*a, **kw)\n        log(f\"deleted {desc}\")\n        return True\n    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n        log(f\"could not delete {desc}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}\")\n        return False\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n@safe\ndef sweep_scheduler():\n    sch = client(\"scheduler\")\n    groups = [\"default\"]\n    try:\n        for page in sch.get_paginator(\"list_schedule_groups\").paginate():\n            groups += [g[\"Name\"] for g in page.get(\"ScheduleGroups\", []) if g[\"Name\"] != \"default\"]\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n        pass\n    for g in groups:\n        try:\n            for page in sch.get_paginator(\"list_schedules\").paginate(GroupName=g):\n                for s in page.get(\"Schedules\", []):\n                    target = (s.get(\"Target\") or {}).get(\"Arn\", \"\")\n                    if owned(s[\"Name\"]) or f\":function:{PREFIX}\" in target or owned(g):\n                        attempt(f\"schedule {g}/{s['Name']}\", sch.delete_schedule, Name=s[\"Name\"], GroupName=g)\n        except Exception as exc:  #
